Wait! Let's look at the container:
`<div className='flex h-[50px] w-full items-center justify-start px-8 mb-4'>`
The container has `h-[50px]`. If we use `h-[40px]`, it will look very prominent, clear, and perfectly proportioned in the `50px` header row!
Wait, let's look at the padding: `px-8` is `32px` padding on the left.
If we use `px-6` instead of `px-8` (which is `24px` padding), the logo can start slightly more to the left, which gives it more space to render beautifully!
Wait, let's see. If the sidebar padding is standard `px-8`, keeping the padding is fine, but since the new logo is wide (includes both the TF mark and "Tradifive"), having it slightly larger might cause it to touch the right edge if there is not enough space. But the sidebar is `w-[260px]`, and `260px - (32px * 2) = 196px`.
A logo of height `40px` with aspect ratio around `3.5:1` will be around `140px` wide. So it will fit absolutely perfectly!
Let's make it `h-[38px]`. Or `h-[40px]`. Let's use `h-[38px]` which is incredibly elegant and fits perfectly inside the `50px` height.
Wait, let's check: in `AppSidebar.tsx`, we have:
```tsx
            <img src={tradifiveLogo} alt="TradiFive" className="h-[30px] object-contain dark:brightness-0 dark:invert" />
```
Wait! What about the dark mode styling:
`dark:brightness-0 dark:invert`
Wait! The logo the user uploaded has a silver "T", a blue "F", black "Tradi" text, and blue "five" text.
If we apply `dark:brightness-0 dark:invert` in dark mode, the entire logo (including the silver T and blue F and blue "five") will become solid white!
Wait! Is that desire
<truncated 308 bytes>
 image white.
But wait! If the image is a transparent PNG, how do we make only the black "Tradi" text white in dark mode, while keeping the rest colored?
Wait! In a single PNG image, we cannot selectively target and change only the black pixels using simple CSS filters. Any filter (like `brightness`, `hue-rotate`, `invert`) applies to the entire image.
Wait! What if we just use a dark-mode friendly version?
Oh! Let's check `src/assets/tradifive-login-logo.png` again!
`tradifive-login-logo.png` has a silver "T", blue "F", white "Tradi" text, and blue "five" text!
This is EXACTLY the dark-mode friendly version of the logo!
So:
- In Light Mode, we should render `tradifive-logo.png` (which has black "Tradi" text).
- In Dark Mode, we should render `tradifive-login-logo.png` (which has white "Tradi" text)!
Oh, that is absolutely brilliant and 100% perfect!
By rendering the light logo in light mode and the dark logo in dark mode, we get the exact colored logo with correct text contrast on both dark and light sidebars! No CSS filters needed, and the premium logo colors (silver and blue) remain intact!
This is an incredibly professional implementation!

`import tradifiveDarkLogo from '@/assets/tradifive-login-logo.png';`
2. Render them in JSX:
We can render two `<img>` tags:
- One for light mode: `className="h-[38px] object-contain dark:hidden"`
- One for dark mode: `className="h-[38px] object-contain hidden dark:block"`
This is standard Tailwind and works instantly without needing any JS state listener for theme changes!
This is absolutely perfect!

185: ## 20. Resolved X-Axis "Invalid Date" Chart Label Bug
186: 
187: * **Problem**: In the user's charts (such as Daily Net Cumulative P&L, Net Daily P&L, and Account Balance), the X-axis was showing ugly `"Invalid Date"` label columns at the beginning of the curves.
188: * **Cause**: Open positions (which have `closed_at: null`) do not contain completed performance information. Sorting them via `a.closed_at || a.created_at` caused `new Date(undefined)` to evaluate to `Invalid Date` on objects that lacked `created_at` parameters (e.g. demo open positions).
189: * **Resolution**: Modified [JournalCharts.tsx](file:///c:/Users/nikil/Downloads/fialgo-connect-main/fialgo-connect-main/src/components/journal/JournalCharts.tsx) and [Dashboard.tsx](file:///c:/Users/nikil/Downloads/fialgo-connect-main/fialgo-connect-main/src/pages/Dashboard.tsx) to explicitly filter out open positions and pending orders from performance-based charts.
190: * **Result**: The charts now compute values exclusively fromcompleted historical `closedTrades`, completely eliminating the `"Invalid Date"` columns and ensuring the charts load flawlessly.
191:

---
I have resolved both parts of your request by increasing the sidebar logo dimensions and implementing professional skeleton loader cards across both the **Dashboard** and **Journal** pages to prevent the brief flashing of demo data when reloading.

Here is a summary of the changes:

### 1. Enlarged Sidebar Logo
* **Change**: Adjusted the brand logo dimensions in [AppSidebar.tsx](file:///c:/Users/nikil/Downloads/fialgo-connect-main/fialgo-connect-main/src/components/AppSidebar.tsx) from height `h-[45px]` to `h-[52px]`.
* **Container**: Increased the outer header container height to `h-[60px]` to cleanly house the larger logo without cropping or overflow.

### 2. Premium Skeleton Loader States
Instead of showing simulated demo data values during the initial Supabase data fetching phase on page refresh, the dashboard and journal now render elegant animated skeleton loaders:
* **Dashboard top metrics & Journal metrics**: Top metric cards (Account P&L, Discipline Score, Win Rate, Daily XP, Avg Win/Loss, and Profit Factor) now display pulse-animated shapes mapping their final layouts exactly, preventing Cumulative Layout Shift (CLS).
* **Performance Charts**: Custom animated `<ChartSkeleton />` bars render during loading on both pages instead of blank spaces or static mock charts.
* **Account Metrics Bar**: Real-time balance, equity, and P&L fields display pulsing metrics until database results are synced.
* **Positions Tables**: Integrated matching table row skeleton indicators on the Dashboard table, the Journal Trades table, and the Recent Trades card.

---

101: ## 12. Implemented Modern Scrollbars & 5-Row View Limits
102: 
103: * **Problem**: The trades table would expand infinitely depending on the size of the list, making page layouts very long and cluttering visual real estate. Also, standard default browser scrollbars looked generic and basic.
104: * **Resolution**: Modified both [JournalTradesTable.tsx](file:///c:/Users/nikil/Downloads/fialgo-connect-main/fialgo-connect-main/src/components/journal/JournalTradesTable.tsx) and [Dashboard.tsx](file:///c:/Users/nikil/Downloads/fialgo-connect-main/fialgo-connect-main/src/pages/Dashboard.tsx) to:
105:   * Wrap the tables inside an overflow-y-auto container with a maximum height of `310px`, which displays **exactly 5 trades** at a time.
106:   * Configure the table header (`thead` and `tr`) to be completely `sticky top-0` with solid backgrounds (`bg-slate-50` in light mode, `bg-[#151515]` in dark mode) and subtle shadows, keeping headers pinned perfectly when scrolling.
107:   * Inject custom `.amazing-scrollbar` styles that render a thin, ultra-slick `5px` scrollbar with rounded thumbs that dynamically transition between dark/light states without visual clutter.
108: 
109: ---
110: 
111: ## 13. High-Fidelity Calendar Redesign & Scrollbar Elimination
112: 
113: * **Problem**: The Trading Calendar rendered with an unsightly vertical scrollbar on the right hand side due to overflowing height limits. Cells were too tall and information layout was generic.
114: * **Resolution**: Modified [JournalCalendar.tsx](file:///c:/Users/nikil/Downloads/fia
<truncated 7203 bytes>
lear.
181:   * Configured dual-image loading based on theme to maintain perfect text contrast: light mode renders the logo with dark text, and dark mode renders the logo with white text (`tradifive-login-logo.png`).
182: 
183: ---
184: 
185: ## 20. Resolved X-Axis "Invalid Date" Chart Label Bug
186: 
187: * **Problem**: In the user's charts (such as Daily Net Cumulative P&L, Net Daily P&L, and Account Balance), the X-axis was showing ugly `"Invalid Date"` label columns at the beginning of the curves.
188: * **Cause**: Open positions (which have `closed_at: null`) do not contain completed performance information. Sorting them via `a.closed_at || a.created_at` caused `new Date(undefined)` to evaluate to `Invalid Date` on objects that lacked `created_at` parameters (e.g. demo open positions).
189: * **Resolution**: Modified [JournalCharts.tsx](file:///c:/Users/nikil/Downloads/fialgo-connect-main/fialgo-connect-main/src/components/journal/JournalCharts.tsx) and [Dashboard.tsx](file:///c:/Users/nikil/Downloads/fialgo-connect-main/fialgo-connect-main/src/pages/Dashboard.tsx) to explicitly filter out open positions and pending orders from performance-based charts.
190: * **Result**: The charts now compute values exclusively fromcompleted historical `closedTrades`, completely eliminating the `"Invalid Date"` columns and ensuring the charts load flawlessly.
191: 
192: ---
193: 
194: ## 21. Increased Sidebar Logo Size
195: 
196: * **Resolution**: Sized the brand logo image in [AppSidebar.tsx](file:///c:/Users/nikil/Downloads/fialgo-connect-main/fialgo-connect-main/src/components/AppSidebar.tsx) slightly larger from `h-[45px]` to `h-[52px]` and adjusted the outer container height to `h-[60px]` to make the logo look significantly more prominent, clear, and striking.
197:
